Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a formal_languages algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 914. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 974/2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the formal_languages decomposition. By minimality, the element a^914 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 974-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the formal_languages structure using the extension principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 199. Each subproblem has a solution by the extension principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
